Title: Живот светога Симеуна и светога Саве (Life of St Simeon and St Sava), written by Domentijan, edited by Đ. Daničić

Publisher: Belgrade: Državna štamparija (title page: У БИОГРАДУ У ДРЖАВНОЈ ШТАМПАРИЈИ)
Publication Date: 1865 (title page)

Source: archive.org item DomentijanZivotSv.SimeonaISv.SaveUredioDjuraDanicic, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-13 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (1,028,169) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected.
